### D3-R6-01 (non-array edge declarations)
Per-node check now REJECTS non-array supportingEdges / contradictingEdges with
EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID. Each array length capped at PER_NODE_EDGE_DECL_CAP=64 (matches
D1 evidence-node-contract); over-cap → ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED.

### D3-R6-02 (edge cap mismatch)
GRAPH_EDGE_INPUT_CAP lowered from 16384 → 1024 to match D2's EDGES_CAP_TOTAL.

### D3-R6-03 (D3 rejected authentic duplicate edges)
Edge expected-set tracking switched from set to MULTI-SET (count-based):
  - Declared edges add +1 to expected count
  - Actual edges consume -1
  - Reserved kind / orphan / count<=0 (extra) / final count>0 (missing) → reject
This matches D2's no-dedup emission semantics: a node declaring `supportingEdges:['n_b','n_b']`
produces 2 identical edges, both consumed by D3's expectation.

### D3-R6-04 NIT (test rigor)

    FNV-1a 32-bit×2 recompute)
  - HRR6-02: per-node edge declaration > 64 rejected
  - HRR6-03-a/b: authentic duplicate edges accepted (multi-set)

# GitHub

## Overview

Use this skill as the umbrella entrypoint for general GitHub work in this plugin. It should decide whether the task stays in repo and PR triage or should be handed off to a more specific review, CI, or publish workflow.

This plugin is intentionally hybrid:

- Prefer the GitHub app from this plugin for repository, issue, pull request, comment, label, reaction, and PR creation workflows.
- Use local `git` and `gh` only when the connector does not cover the job well, especially for current-branch PR discovery, branch creation, commit and push, `gh auth status`, and GitHub Actions log inspection.
- Keep connector state and local checkout context aligned. If the request is about the current branch, resolve the local repo and branch before acting.

Once the intent is clear, route to the specialist skill immediately and do not keep broad GitHub triage in scope longer than needed.

## Connector-First Responsibilities

Handle these directly in this skill when the request does not need a narrower specialist workflow:

- repository orientation once the repo, PR, issue, or local checkout is identified
- recent PR or issue triage
- PR metadata summaries
- PR patch inspection
- PR comments, labels, and reactions
- issue lookup and summarization
- PR creation after a branch is already pushed

Prefer the GitHub app from this plugin for those flows because it provides structured PR, issue, and review-adjacent data without depending on a local checkout. If the repository is not already identifiable from the user request or local git context, ask for the repo instead of pretending there is a repo-search flow that may not exist.

## Routing Rules

1. Resolve the operating context first:
   - If the user provides a repository, PR number, issue number, or URL, use that.
   - If the request is about "this branch" or "the current PR", resolve local git context and use `gh` only as needed to discover the branch PR.
   - If the repository is still ambiguous after local inspection, ask for the repo identifier.
2. Classify the request before taking action:
   - `repo or PR triage`: summarize PRs, issues, patches, comments, labels, reactions, or repository state
   - `review follow-up`: unresolved review threads, requested changes, or inline review feedback
   - `CI debugging`: failing checks, Actions logs, or CI root-cause analysis
   - `publish changes`: create or switch branches, stage changes, commit, push, and open a draft PR
3. Route to the specialist skill as soon as the category is clear:
   - Review comments and requested changes: `../gh-address-comments/SKILL.md`
   - Failing GitHub Actions checks: `../gh-fix-ci/SKILL.md`
   - Commit, push, and open PR: `../yeet/SKILL.md`
4. Keep the hybrid model consistent after routing:
   - connector first for PR and issue data
   - local `git` and `gh` only for the specific gaps the connector does not cover

## Default Workflow

1. Resolve repository and item scope.
2. Gather structured PR or issue context through the GitHub app from this plugin.
3. Decide whether the task stays in connector-backed triage or needs a specialist skill.
4. Route immediately when the work becomes review follow-up, CI debugging, or publish workflow.
5. End with a clear summary of what was inspected, what changed, and what remains.

## Output Expectations

- For triage requests, return a concise summary of the repository, PR, or issue state and the next likely action.
- For mixed requests, tell the user which specialist path you are taking and why.
- For connector-backed write actions, restate the exact PR, issue, label, or reaction target before applying the change.
- Never imply that GitHub Actions logs are available through the connector alone. That remains a `gh` workflow.

## Examples

- "Use GitHub to summarize the open PRs in this repo and tell me what needs attention."
- "Help with this PR."
- "Review the latest comments on PR 482 and tell me what is actionable."
- "Debug the failing checks on this branch."
- "Commit these changes, push them, and open a draft PR."

   // Step 0 entry guard recomputes these against the live HI api and rejects the call if the

   function _intrinsicsIntact() {

     // any caller code runs). Validation-path security relies on the HI captured wrappers — the

   function _crAtLeast(a, b) {

     var ia = _crIndex(a); var ib = _crIndex(b);

   // Hash function MUST be byte-equivalent to D2 evidence-graph.js _hash64 (FNV-1a 32-bit twice,
   // concatenated: hash32(s) + hash32(s + '|' + s.length)). This is the only way an authoritative
   // graphId recompute on the D3 side can match the D2-produced value. Re-using BigInt FNV-1a 64-bit
   // would produce a different hash and reject every authentic graph as "forged".
   function _toHex8(h) {
     var hexChars = '0123456789abcdef';
     var out = '';
     var x = h >>> 0;
     for (var i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
       var nibble = (x >>> (i * 4)) & 0xF;
       out += hexChars[nibble];
     }
     return out;

   function _countIndependentGroups(matched, graph) {
     // Map nodeId → correlationGroupId (a node may belong to at most one correlation group). A node
     // not in any correlation group counts as its own independent unit.
     var memberToGroupId = _newSet();

       }
     }
     var groupSeen = _newSet();
     var soloCount = 0;
     var matchedGroupIds = [];

   }
 
   function _credibilityWeightFor(c) {
     if (c === 'measured') return 5;
     if (c === 'derived') return 3;
     if (c === 'heuristic') return 1;
     return 0;
   }
   function _aggregateCredibility(matched) {
     var hasMeasured = false, hasDerived = false, hasHeuristic = false, hasSynthetic = false;
     for (var i = 0; i < matched.length; i++) {
       var c = matched[i].credibility;
       if (c === 'measured') hasMeasured = true;
       else if (c === 'derived') hasDerived = true;
       else if (c === 'heuristic') hasHeuristic = true;
       else if (c === 'synthetic') hasSynthetic = true;
     }
     if (hasMeasured) return 'Measured';
     if (hasDerived) return 'Derived';
     if (hasHeuristic) return 'Heuristic';
     if (hasSynthetic) return 'Heuristic';
     return 'Unavailable';
   }
   function _classifyConfidence(supportingGroups, soloCount, contradictionCount, supportCount, aggregateCred, rule) {

+    // closure). Any node reaching this function is already fresh-enough per policy.
     var contradictionRatio = (supportCount === 0) ? 0 : (contradictionCount / (supportCount + contradictionCount));
 
     // Block if cannotConclude reasons present at the graph level; engine adds it externally.
     // Block if credibility floor not met.

       return { state: 'low', status: 'inconclusive' };
     }
     if (totalIndependent < rule.minIndependentGroups) {
       return { state: 'insufficient_evidence', status: 'inconclusive' };
     }
     if (contradictionRatio > rule.maxContradictionRatio) {

     }

     }

     }
     return { state: 'low', status: 'supported' };
   }
   function _computeConfidenceScore(matched, contradictionCount, totalIndependent) {
     var base = totalIndependent * 25;
     var credBoost = 0;
     for (var i = 0; i < matched.length; i++) {
       credBoost += _credibilityWeightFor(matched[i].credibility);
     }
     var contraPenalty = contradictionCount * 20;
     var raw = base + credBoost - contraPenalty;
     if (raw < SCORE_MIN) raw = SCORE_MIN;
     if (raw > SCORE_MAX) raw = SCORE_MAX;
     return HI.safeMathFloor(raw);
   }
   function _aggregateLimitations(matched, rule) {
     var seen = _newSet();
     var out = [];
     // Add rule-required limitations
     for (var ri = 0; ri < rule.requiredLimitations.length; ri++) {
       var rcode = rule.requiredLimitations[ri];
       if (RC.isReasonCode(rcode) && !_setHas(seen, rcode)) { _setAdd(seen, rcode); _arrPush(out, rcode); }
     }
     // Union of all matched-node limitations
     for (var ni = 0; ni < matched.length; ni++) {

       if (!_isPlainArray(n.limitations)) continue;
       for (var li = 0; li < n.limitations.length; li++) {
         var l = n.limitations[li];
         if (RC.isReasonCode(l) && !_setHas(seen, l)) { _setAdd(seen, l); _arrPush(out, l); }
       }
     }
     // Heuristic credibility → must declare LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY
     var anyHeuristic = false;
     for (var i = 0; i < matched.length; i++) {
       if (matched[i].credibility === 'heuristic') { anyHeuristic = true; break; }
     }
     if (anyHeuristic && !_setHas(seen, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY)) {
       _setAdd(seen, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY);
       _arrPush(out, CODES.LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY);
     }
     var anySynthetic = false;
     for (var j = 0; j < matched.length; j++) {
       if (matched[j].credibility === 'synthetic') { anySynthetic = true; break; }
     }
     if (anySynthetic && !_setHas(seen, CODES.LIMITATION_SYNTHETIC_ONLY)) {
       _setAdd(seen, CODES.LIMITATION_SYNTHETIC_ONLY);
       _arrPush(out, CODES.LIMITATION_SYNTHETIC_ONLY);
     }
     HI.safeArraySort(out, _strcmp);
     if (out.length > REASON_CODE_PER_HYPOTHESIS_CAP) {
       out = HI.safeArraySlice(out);
       out.length = REASON_CODE_PER_HYPOTHESIS_CAP;
       out = HI.safeArraySlice(out);  // structurally safe re-allocation
     }
     return out;
   }

+// ---------- Section H6 — Codex D3 R6 closure tests (D3-R6-01..05) -------------------------------
+// Reuse D2/D3's FNV-1a 32-bit-twice hash to construct HASH-VALID test fixtures (graphs whose
+// `graphId` field matches the recomputed value on their actual contents). Without this we can
+// only test rejection-via-hash-mismatch, which doesn't exercise downstream checks.
+console.log('Section H6 — Codex D3 R6 closures (hash-valid fixtures)');
+function _hash32_for_test(s) {
+  var h = 0x811c9dc5;
+  var len = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
+  for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
+    h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
+    h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
+  }
+  var hexChars = '0123456789abcdef';
+  var out = '';
+  for (var j = 7; j >= 0; j--) out += hexChars[(h >>> (j * 4)) & 0xF];
+  return out;
+}
+function _hash64_for_test(s) {
+  return _hash32_for_test(s) + _hash32_for_test(s + '|' + s.length);
+}

codex
The core R6 changes are present, but the manifest’s separate `caps` field still advertises `GRAPH_EDGE_INPUT_CAP=16384` while production now enforces 1024. I’m treating that as a new synchronization vector and continuing with executable boundary cases and hash equivalence.

   168	  // Codex D3 R2 R2-03 closure: directive §5.18 mandates staleness-based invalidation.
   169	  // Default cutoff is 30 days. Caller can override via opts.maxAgeMs; reference time MUST
   170	  // be supplied via opts.clock() returning ISO 8601 (preferred) or opts.referenceNowMs.
   171	  var DEFAULT_MAX_AGE_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
   172	
   173	  // Confidence state ladder (semantic primary; numeric score is auxiliary).
   174	  var CONFIDENCE_STATES = HI.deepFreeze(['not_computed', 'insufficient_evidence', 'low', 'moderate', 'high']);
   175	  // Status ladder.
   176	  var HYPOTHESIS_STATUSES = HI.deepFreeze(['supported', 'contradicted', 'inconclusive', 'blocked']);
   177	
   178	  // Confidence score bounds — integer 0..100. Avoids float ambiguity entirely.
   179	  var SCORE_MIN = 0;
   180	  var SCORE_MAX = 100;
   181	
   182	  // Conclusion credibility ladder (per credibility-contract).
   183	  var CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY_ORDER = HI.deepFreeze(['Physics', 'Model', 'Measured', 'Derived', 'Heuristic', 'Unavailable']);
   184	  function _crIndex(c) { return HI.safeArrayIndexOf(CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY_ORDER, c); }
   185	  function _crAtLeast(a, b) {
   186	    // a, b in CONCLUSION_CREDIBILITY_ORDER. lower index = stronger. a is at-least-as-strong as b iff a's index ≤ b's index.
   187	    var ia = _crIndex(a); var ib = _crIndex(b);
   188	    if (ia === -1 || ib === -1) return false;
   189	    return ia <= ib;
   190	  }
   191	
   192	  // ID grammar mirrors D1 hypothesis-contract.
   193	  var ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/;
   194	  var ID_FORBIDDEN_RE = /(\.\.|[\/\\]|^\.)/;
   195	
   196	  // ---------- Helper utilities (HI-only) --------------------------------------------------------
   197	  function _isNonEmptyString(v) {
   198	    return typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0;
   199	  }
   200	  function _isFiniteNumber(v) {
   201	    return typeof v === 'number' && HI.safeNumberIsFinite(v) === true;
   202	  }
   203	  function _isInteger(v) {
   204	    return HI.safeNumberIsInteger(v) === true;
   205	  }
   206	  function _isPlainObject(v) {
   207	    return HI.safeIsPlainShape(v) === 'plain-object';
   208	  }
   209	  function _isPlainArray(v) {
   210	    return HI.safeIsPlainShape(v) === 'plain-array';
   230	    HI.safeDefineDataProperty(setObj, key, true);
   231	  }
   232	  function _newSet() {
   233	    return HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   234	  }
   235	  // Hash function MUST be byte-equivalent to D2 evidence-graph.js _hash64 (FNV-1a 32-bit twice,
   236	  // concatenated: hash32(s) + hash32(s + '|' + s.length)). This is the only way an authoritative
   237	  // graphId recompute on the D3 side can match the D2-produced value. Re-using BigInt FNV-1a 64-bit
   238	  // would produce a different hash and reject every authentic graph as "forged".
   239	  function _toHex8(h) {
   240	    var hexChars = '0123456789abcdef';
   241	    var out = '';
   242	    var x = h >>> 0;
   243	    for (var i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
   244	      var nibble = (x >>> (i * 4)) & 0xF;
   245	      out += hexChars[nibble];
   246	    }
   247	    return out;
   248	  }
   249	  function _hash32(s) {
   250	    var h = 0x811c9dc5;
   251	    var len = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
   252	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   253	      h ^= HI.safeStringCharCodeAt(s, i);
   254	      h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
   255	    }
   256	    return _toHex8(h);
   257	  }
   258	  function _hashFNV64Hex(s) {
   259	    var slen = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
   260	    return _hash32(s) + _hash32(s + '|' + slen);
   261	  }
   262	  // ---------- Authoritative graph verification --------------------------------------------------
   263	  // The graph payload that produced graphId in D2 (see r3-0d-evidence-graph.js Step 16, ~line
   264	  // 1308): { caseAssociation, nodes (projected), edges, correlationGroups (projected) }. To verify
   265	  // authority, we project the same fields out of the suspect graph and recompute the hash with
   266	  // the same prefix ('graphid|v1|'). Mismatch → fabrication.
   267	  function _projectIdPayload(graph) {
   268	    var projectedNodes = HI.safeArrayMap(graph.nodes, function (n) {
   269	      var idPart = {
   270	        caseId: n.identity.caseId,
   271	        sessionId: n.identity.sessionId,
   272	        lapId: n.identity.lapId == null ? null : n.identity.lapId,
   273	        sourceId: n.identity.sourceId,
   274	        sourceVersion: n.identity.sourceVersion,
   275	      };
   276	      var obsPart = {
   277	        kind: n.observation.kind,
   278	        channel: n.observation.channel == null ? null : n.observation.channel,
   279	        i18nKey: n.observation.i18nKey,
   280	        params: n.observation.params == null ? null : n.observation.params,
   281	      };
   282	      return {
   283	        nodeId: n.nodeId,
   284	        category: n.category,
   285	        credibility: n.credibility,
   286	        provenance: n.provenance,
   287	        availability: n.availability,
   288	        confidence: { state: (n.confidence && n.confidence.state) ? n.confidence.state : null },
   289	        identity: idPart,
   290	        observation: obsPart,
   291	        limitations: n.limitations,
   292	        supportingEdges: n.supportingEdges,
   293	        contradictingEdges: n.contradictingEdges,
   294	      };
   295	    });
   296	    var projectedCgs = HI.safeArrayMap(graph.correlationGroups, function (g) {
   297	      return {
   298	        correlationGroupId: g.correlationGroupId,
   299	        memberNodeIds: g.memberNodeIds,
   300	        independenceWeight: g.independenceWeight,
   301	      };
   302	    });
   303	    return {
   304	      caseAssociation: {
   305	        caseId: graph.caseAssociation.caseId,
   306	        sessionId: graph.caseAssociation.sessionId,
   307	        lapId: graph.caseAssociation.lapId == null ? null : graph.caseAssociation.lapId,
   308	      },
   309	      nodes: projectedNodes,
   310	      edges: graph.edges,
   311	      correlationGroups: projectedCgs,
   312	    };
   313	  }
   314	
   315	  function _recomputeGraphId(graph) {
   316	    var idPayload = _projectIdPayload(graph);
   317	    var serialized = HI.stableStringify(idPayload);
   318	    return 'graph_' + _hashFNV64Hex('graphid|v' + graph.schemaVersion + '|' + serialized);
   319	  }
   320	
   321	  /**
   322	   * Validate that the supplied object IS a D2 authoritative graph snapshot. Returns
   323	   * { valid:true } or { valid:false, reasonCodes:[...] }.
   324	   *
   325	   * Checks (in order):
   360	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   361	    }
   362	    if (!_isPlainObject(g)) {
   363	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   364	    }
   365	    // Post-clone audit — the clone IS plain (no Proxy), so this is meaningful: catches
   366	    // class-instance / Array-subclass / Symbol-key / accessor / sparse / non-finite-number
   367	    // smuggling through structuredClone's permissive deep-clone semantics.
   368	    if (HI.deepOriginalShapeAudit(g) !== true) {
   369	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   370	    }
   371	    if (g.schemaVersion !== 1) {
   372	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA] };
   373	    }
   374	    if (!_isNonEmptyString(g.graphId)) {
   375	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   376	    }
   377	    if (g.graphId.length > 64 || !HI.safeRegExpTest(/^graph_[0-9a-f]{16}$/, g.graphId)) {
   378	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   379	    }
   380	    if (!_isPlainObject(g.caseAssociation)) {
   381	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   382	    }
   383	    if (!_isNonEmptyString(g.caseAssociation.caseId) || !_isNonEmptyString(g.caseAssociation.sessionId)) {
   384	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   385	    }
   386	    if (!_isPlainObject(g.sessionAssociation)
   387	        || g.sessionAssociation.sessionId !== g.caseAssociation.sessionId) {
   388	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH] };
   389	    }
   390	    if (!_isPlainArray(g.nodes)) {
   391	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   392	    }
   393	    if (g.nodes.length > GRAPH_NODE_INPUT_CAP) {
   394	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   395	    }
   396	    if (!_isPlainArray(g.edges)) {
   397	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   398	    }
   399	    if (g.edges.length > GRAPH_EDGE_INPUT_CAP) {
   400	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.GRAPH_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   401	    }
   402	    if (!_isPlainArray(g.correlationGroups)) {
   403	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   404	    }
   405	    if (!_isPlainArray(g.limitations)) {
   406	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   407	    }
   408	    if (!_isPlainArray(g.cannotConclude)) {
   409	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   410	    }
   411	
   412	    // Per-node identity scope + D2 invariant re-validation. Codex D3 R1/R2 closures:
   413	    // the graphId hash is a diagnostic identifier, not an integrity proof; D3 MUST independently
   414	    // re-enforce every D2 invariant on the snapshot it has just cloned. We do NOT trust the
   415	    // hashed projection alone — the projection itself is not the full graph schema.
   416	    var caseId = g.caseAssociation.caseId;
   417	    var sessionId = g.caseAssociation.sessionId;
   418	    var caseLapId = (g.caseAssociation.lapId == null) ? null : g.caseAssociation.lapId;
   419	    var seenNodeIds = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   420	    var nodeIdSet = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   421	    // Build the expected correlation partition from node identities. Two nodes share a
   422	    // correlation group iff they share (caseId|sessionId|lapId|sourceId). This mirrors D2's
   423	    // _correlationGroupKey logic. Codex D3 R2 R2-01 closure: D3 must reconstruct and verify the
   424	    // partition itself, not just validate the listed groups in isolation.
   425	    var expectedGroupBuckets = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();  // key → { memberNodeIds: [...] }
   426	    var nodeCorrelationKey = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();    // nodeId → key
   427	
   428	    for (var i = 0; i < g.nodes.length; i++) {
   429	      var n = g.nodes[i];
   430	      if (!_isPlainObject(n)) {
   431	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   432	      }
   433	      // Codex D3 R4 D3-R4-01 closure: delegate FULL SourceIdentity validation to the D1 contract.
   434	      // SI.validateSourceIdentity enforces: (a) closed key set {caseId, sessionId, lapId, sourceId,
   435	      // sourceVersion, freshness} via Reflect.ownKeys allowlist (rejects extra keys + Symbol keys);
   436	      // (b) caseId/sessionId/sourceId/sourceVersion/freshness mandatory non-empty strings;
   437	      // (c) byte caps (≤512) on ALL string fields including caseId/sessionId; (d) lapId optional but
   438	      // if present must be null or non-empty string + byte cap; (e) freshness must match the
   439	      // contract's ISO 8601 grammar (T separator + Z or ±HH:MM offset). This single delegation
   440	      // replaces several inline checks that were partial.
   441	      var sidCheck = SI.validateSourceIdentity(n.identity);
   442	      if (sidCheck.valid !== true) {
   443	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: (sidCheck && sidCheck.reasonCodes) ? sidCheck.reasonCodes : [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   444	      }
   445	      if (!_isPlainObject(n.identity)) {
   446	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   447	      }
   448	      // Codex D3 R2 R2-02 closure: enforce ID_RE grammar + forbidden pattern + byte cap on
   449	      // every node.nodeId. _isNonEmptyString alone admitted '../bad' style IDs.
   450	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(n.nodeId)) {
   451	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_MISSING_ID] };
   452	      }
   453	      if (HI.safeRegExpTest(ID_FORBIDDEN_RE, n.nodeId) || !HI.safeRegExpTest(ID_RE, n.nodeId)) {
   454	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN] };
   455	      }
   456	      if (_byteLength(n.nodeId) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) {
   457	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   458	      }
   459	      // Codex D3 R1 RN-02 closure: D2 invariant — no duplicate nodeId.
   460	      if (HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(seenNodeIds, n.nodeId)) {
   461	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_DUPLICATE_ID] };
   462	      }
   463	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(seenNodeIds, n.nodeId, true);
   464	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(nodeIdSet, n.nodeId, true);
   465	
   466	      if (n.identity.caseId !== caseId) {
   467	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_CASE_MISMATCH] };
   468	      }
   469	      if (n.identity.sessionId !== sessionId) {
   470	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_SESSION_MISMATCH] };
   471	      }
   472	      var nodeLapId = (n.identity.lapId == null) ? null : n.identity.lapId;
   473	      if (caseLapId !== null && nodeLapId !== null && caseLapId !== nodeLapId) {
   474	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_LAP_MISMATCH] };
   475	      }
   476	      if (n.identity.sourceId === 'imported_summary' && n.credibility === 'measured') {
   477	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_IMPORTED_SUMMARY_ELEVATION_FORBIDDEN] };
   478	      }
   479	      // Codex D3 R3 R3-04 closure: enforce full SourceIdentity invariants — every identity field
   480	      // that D2 requires non-empty MUST be non-empty + within byte cap. sourceVersion=null /
   481	      // sourceId="" / lapId="" / freshness="" all sneak past the basic scope checks if not
   482	      // explicitly rejected here.
   483	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(n.identity.sourceId)) {
   484	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   485	      }
   486	      if (_byteLength(n.identity.sourceId) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) {
   487	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   488	      }
   489	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(n.identity.sourceVersion)) {
   490	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_VERSION_MISSING] };
   491	      }
   492	      if (_byteLength(n.identity.sourceVersion) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) {
   493	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   494	      }
   495	      if (nodeLapId !== null) {
   496	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(nodeLapId)) {
   497	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID] };
   498	        }
   499	        if (_byteLength(nodeLapId) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) {
   500	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   501	        }
   502	      }
   503	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(n.identity.freshness)) {
   504	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_FRESHNESS_STALE] };
   505	      }
   506	      if (_byteLength(n.identity.freshness) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) {
   507	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   508	      }
   509	      var freshMs = _isoToMs(n.identity.freshness);
   510	      if (freshMs === null) {
   511	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_FRESHNESS_STALE] };
   512	      }
   513	      // Codex D3 R2 R2-03 closure: freshness enforcement is now MANDATORY. refNowMs must be
   514	      // available (resolved from opts.clock or opts.referenceNowMs). maxAgeMs has a default
   515	      // (DEFAULT_MAX_AGE_MS). If no reference time is available we fail-closed before this
   516	      // function is even called — see buildHypothesisSet entry.
   517	      if ((refNowMs - freshMs) > maxAgeMs) {
   518	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_FRESHNESS_STALE] };
   519	      }
   520	      if (freshMs > (refNowMs + (5 * 60 * 1000))) {
   521	        // Reject future-dated freshness (>5min in the future) — clock-skew tolerance only.
   522	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_FRESHNESS_STALE] };
   523	      }
   524	
   525	      // Codex D3 R3 R3-01 closure: collision-free correlation key. Use D2's exact encoding
   526	      // (r3-0d-evidence-graph.js:_correlationGroupCanonical) - HI.stableStringify each
   527	      // component so JSON-string escaping eliminates ambiguity (caseId="a|b" cannot collide
   528	      // with lapId containing "|"). The '|' delimiter sits BETWEEN JSON-quoted strings.
   529	      var corrKey = HI.stableStringify(caseId)
   530	        + '|' + HI.stableStringify(sessionId)
   531	        + '|' + HI.stableStringify(nodeLapId == null ? null : nodeLapId)
   532	        + '|' + HI.stableStringify(n.identity.sourceId);
   533	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(nodeCorrelationKey, n.nodeId, corrKey);
   534	      var bucketDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedGroupBuckets, corrKey);
   535	      if (bucketDesc) {
   536	        _arrPush(bucketDesc.value, n.nodeId);
   537	      } else {
   538	        HI.safeDefineDataProperty(expectedGroupBuckets, corrKey, [n.nodeId]);
   539	      }
   540	    }
   541	
   542	    // Codex D3 R2 R2-01 closure: validate that the graph's correlationGroups EXACTLY match the
   543	    // expected partition derived from node identities. EVERY bucket (singleton or multi-member)
   544	    // MUST appear as exactly one correlationGroup with the same memberNodeIds (sorted) and
   545	    // independenceWeight = exact 1 / count (Object.is equality — Codex D3 R2 R2-02 closure
   546	    // rejects NaN/Infinity). This mirrors D2's behavior: D2 emits one correlation group per
   547	    // distinct (case|session|lap|sourceId) identity tuple.
   548	    var expectedKeys = HI.safeOwnKeys(expectedGroupBuckets) || [];
   549	    var expectedAllKeys = [];
   550	    for (var eki = 0; eki < expectedKeys.length; eki++) {
   551	      var ek = expectedKeys[eki];
   552	      var dEK = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedGroupBuckets, ek);
   553	      if (dEK && _isPlainArray(dEK.value) && dEK.value.length >= 1) {
   554	        _arrPush(expectedAllKeys, ek);
   555	        HI.safeArraySort(dEK.value, _strcmp);
   556	      }
   557	    }
   558	    HI.safeArraySort(expectedAllKeys, _strcmp);
   559	    if (g.correlationGroups.length !== expectedAllKeys.length) {
   560	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   561	    }
   562	    var matchedKey = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   563	    var seenGroupId = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   564	    var globalMemberSeen = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   565	    for (var cgi = 0; cgi < g.correlationGroups.length; cgi++) {
   566	      var cg = g.correlationGroups[cgi];
   567	      if (!_isPlainObject(cg) || !_isPlainArray(cg.memberNodeIds) || cg.memberNodeIds.length === 0) {
   568	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   569	      }
   570	      // Codex D3 R3 R3-02 closure: correlationGroupId must conform to D2's grammar
   571	      // (`corr_<16hex>`) AND must be unique across g.correlationGroups. Two distinct groups
   572	      // sharing the same ID would previously bypass downstream dedup.
   573	      if (!_isNonEmptyString(cg.correlationGroupId)) {
   574	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   575	      }
   576	      if (HI.safeRegExpTest(ID_FORBIDDEN_RE, cg.correlationGroupId)
   577	          || !HI.safeRegExpTest(/^corr_[0-9a-f]{16}$/, cg.correlationGroupId)) {
   578	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN] };
   579	      }
   580	      if (HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(seenGroupId, cg.correlationGroupId)) {
   581	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   582	      }
   583	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(seenGroupId, cg.correlationGroupId, true);
   584	
   585	      // All member IDs share the same expected correlation key.
   586	      var firstMember = cg.memberNodeIds[0];
   587	      var keyDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeCorrelationKey, firstMember);
   588	      if (!keyDesc) {
   589	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   590	      }
   591	      var keyForThisGroup = keyDesc.value;
   592	      var bucketDescForKey = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedGroupBuckets, keyForThisGroup);
   593	      if (!bucketDescForKey) {
   594	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   595	      }
   596	      var expectedMembers = bucketDescForKey.value;
   597	      if (HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(matchedKey, keyForThisGroup)) {
   598	        // The same correlation key appears twice in correlationGroups — invalid.
   599	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   600	      }
   601	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(matchedKey, keyForThisGroup, true);
   602	      if (cg.memberNodeIds.length !== expectedMembers.length) {
   603	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   604	      }
   605	      // Verify members are exactly the expected set (sorted equality).
   606	      var sortedActual = HI.safeArraySlice(cg.memberNodeIds);
   607	      HI.safeArraySort(sortedActual, _strcmp);
   608	      for (var mi = 0; mi < sortedActual.length; mi++) {
   609	        if (sortedActual[mi] !== expectedMembers[mi]) {
   610	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   611	        }
   612	        if (!HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, sortedActual[mi])) {
   613	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   614	        }
   615	        if (HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(globalMemberSeen, sortedActual[mi])) {
   616	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   617	        }
   618	        HI.safeDefineDataProperty(globalMemberSeen, sortedActual[mi], true);
   619	      }
   620	      // Codex D3 R3 R3-02 closure (cont.): correlationGroupId must equal D2's recompute
   621	      // ('corr_' + _hash64('corr|' + canonical)). Binds the supplied ID to actual cluster
   622	      // contents — caller cannot rename group IDs to forge dedup.
   623	      var expectedGroupId = 'corr_' + _hashFNV64Hex('corr|' + keyForThisGroup);
   624	      if (cg.correlationGroupId !== expectedGroupId) {
   625	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   626	      }
   627	      // Exact Object.is(weight, 1/count) — rejects NaN, Infinity, or any float drift.
   628	      if (!_exactlyEqual(cg.independenceWeight, 1 / cg.memberNodeIds.length)) {
   629	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_CORRELATED_METRICS_DOUBLECOUNT] };
   630	      }
   631	    }
   632	
   633	    // Codex D3 R5 D3-R5-01 closure: reconstruct the EXACT edge set D2 emits from the
   634	    // sanitized node fields + correlation groups, then require g.edges to match it as a
   635	    // set. D2 emits exactly three edge kinds:
   636	    //   - 'supports'         from each node.supportingEdges entry
   637	    //   - 'contradicts'      from each node.contradictingEdges entry
   638	    //   - 'correlated_with'  from pairs of correlation-group members (sorted lo→hi)
   639	    // The closed enum {supports, contradicts, derived_from, correlated_with, invalidates}
   640	    // describes the universe of LEGAL kinds, but `derived_from` and `invalidates` are
   641	    // RESERVED — no D2 producer emits them. D3 rejects any graph claiming a reserved kind
   642	    // because such a graph cannot have come from D2.
   643	    var EDGE_KIND_EMITTED = ['supports', 'contradicts', 'correlated_with'];
   644	    // Codex D3 R6 D3-R6-03 closure: D2 emits edges WITHOUT deduplication — a node declaring
   645	    // the same id twice in supportingEdges produces 2 identical {from,to,supports} edges in
   646	    // graph.edges. D3 was strictly set-equal which would reject authentic D2 output. Switch
   647	    // to MULTI-SET (count-based) tracking: each declared edge increments expected count;
   648	    // each actual edge decrements; final counts must all be zero.
   649	    var expectedEdgeCount = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   650	    function _expectedEdgeKey(from, to, kind) { return from + '|' + to + '|' + kind; }
   651	    function _addExpectedEdge(from, to, kind) {
   652	      var k = _expectedEdgeKey(from, to, kind);
   653	      var d = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedEdgeCount, k);
   654	      var cur = (d && typeof d.value === 'number') ? d.value : 0;
   655	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(expectedEdgeCount, k, cur + 1);
   656	    }
   657	    // supports + contradicts from node declarations.
   658	    // Codex D3 R6 D3-R6-01 closure: each declaration field MUST be a plain array with bounded
   659	    // string entries; silent skip on non-array was an authority bypass.
   660	    for (var nei = 0; nei < g.nodes.length; nei++) {
   661	      var ne = g.nodes[nei];
   662	      if (!_isPlainArray(ne.supportingEdges)) {
   663	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   664	      }
   665	      if (ne.supportingEdges.length > PER_NODE_EDGE_DECL_CAP) {
   666	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   667	      }
   668	      for (var sj = 0; sj < ne.supportingEdges.length; sj++) {
   669	        var sId = ne.supportingEdges[sj];
   670	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(sId) || !HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, sId)) {
   671	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   672	        }
   673	        if (sId === ne.nodeId) {
   674	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE] };
   675	        }
   676	        _addExpectedEdge(ne.nodeId, sId, 'supports');
   677	      }
   678	      if (!_isPlainArray(ne.contradictingEdges)) {
   679	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   680	      }
   681	      if (ne.contradictingEdges.length > PER_NODE_EDGE_DECL_CAP) {
   682	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   683	      }
   684	      for (var cj = 0; cj < ne.contradictingEdges.length; cj++) {
   685	        var cId = ne.contradictingEdges[cj];
   686	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(cId) || !HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, cId)) {
   687	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   688	        }
   689	        if (cId === ne.nodeId) {
   690	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE] };
   691	        }
   692	        _addExpectedEdge(ne.nodeId, cId, 'contradicts');
   693	      }
   694	    }
   695	    // correlated_with from groups with ≥2 members (pairwise lo→hi)
   696	    for (var cgj = 0; cgj < g.correlationGroups.length; cgj++) {
   697	      var cgM = g.correlationGroups[cgj].memberNodeIds;
   698	      if (!_isPlainArray(cgM) || cgM.length < 2) continue;
   699	      var sortedM = HI.safeArraySlice(cgM);
   700	      HI.safeArraySort(sortedM, _strcmp);
   701	      for (var mai = 0; mai < sortedM.length; mai++) {
   702	        for (var mbj = mai + 1; mbj < sortedM.length; mbj++) {
   703	          _addExpectedEdge(sortedM[mai], sortedM[mbj], 'correlated_with');
   704	        }
   705	      }
   706	    }
   707	    // Verify g.edges matches the expected MULTI-SET — every actual edge consumes one count;
   708	    // any reserved kind / orphan / not-in-expected / over-consumed → reject; final counts
   709	    // must all be zero (no missing expected edge).
   710	    for (var ei = 0; ei < g.edges.length; ei++) {
   711	      var e = g.edges[ei];
   712	      if (!_isPlainObject(e) || !_isNonEmptyString(e.from) || !_isNonEmptyString(e.to) || !_isNonEmptyString(e.kind)) {
   713	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   714	      }
   715	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(EDGE_KIND_EMITTED, e.kind) === -1) {
   716	        // Reserved kind (derived_from / invalidates) OR unknown kind — rejected.
   717	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   718	      }
   719	      if (!HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, e.from) || !HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, e.to)) {
   720	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   721	      }
   722	      var eKey = _expectedEdgeKey(e.from, e.to, e.kind);
   723	      var eDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedEdgeCount, eKey);
   724	      var eCur = (eDesc && typeof eDesc.value === 'number') ? eDesc.value : 0;
   725	      if (eCur <= 0) {
   726	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   727	      }
   728	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(expectedEdgeCount, eKey, eCur - 1);
   729	    }
   730	    // Any expected edge not consumed → caller stripped an edge D2 would emit.
   731	    var remainingKeys = HI.safeOwnKeys(expectedEdgeCount) || [];
   732	    for (var rki = 0; rki < remainingKeys.length; rki++) {
   733	      var rkDesc = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedEdgeCount, remainingKeys[rki]);
   734	      if (rkDesc && typeof rkDesc.value === 'number' && rkDesc.value > 0) {
   735	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   736	      }
   737	    }
   738	    // Preserve original endpoint-existence check (defensive):
   739	    for (var lei = 0; lei < g.edges.length; lei++) {
   740	      var le = g.edges[lei];
   741	      if (!HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, le.from) || !HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, le.to)) {
   742	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   743	      }
   744	    }
   745	
   746	    // Recompute graphId — independent authority verification. NOTE: the graphId only hashes
   747	    // (caseAssociation, nodes-projection, edges, correlationGroups). Other fields like
   748	    // graph.cannotConclude / graph.limitations / graph.provenance / graph.createdAt are NOT
   749	    // bound to graphId — Codex D3 R1 RN-01 closure: D3's output must NOT consume those fields
   750	    // from the input graph (they are re-derived from rule firing below).
   751	    var recomputed;
   752	    try { recomputed = _recomputeGraphId(g); }
   753	    catch (e) { return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] }; }
   754	    if (recomputed !== g.graphId) {
   755	      return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.HYPOTHESIS_AUTHORITY_FORGED] };
   756	    }
   757	
   758	    // Deep-freeze the cloned snapshot before returning so downstream code receives an
   759	    // immutable view (the clone was mutable until now).
   760	    return { valid: true, graph: HI.deepFreeze(g) };
   761	  }
   762	
   763	  // ---------- Rule registry (closed) ------------------------------------------------------------
   764	  // Each rule deterministically tests against the sanitized graph and may produce zero or one
   765	  // hypothesis. New rules require new module load; runtime registration is NOT supported.
  1010	      var contraId = null;
  1011	      if (_setHas(matchedIds, e.from) && !_setHas(matchedIds, e.to)) {
  1012	        contraId = e.to;
  1013	      } else if (_setHas(matchedIds, e.to) && !_setHas(matchedIds, e.from)) {
  1014	        // Reverse-direction fallback for symmetry.
  1015	        contraId = e.from;
  1016	      } else if (_setHas(matchedIds, e.from) && _setHas(matchedIds, e.to)) {
  1017	        // Both endpoints matched — pick the non-self side (matched contradicts another matched).
  1018	        // Skip if same nodeId (self-edge, defensive — D2 disallows but defend anyway).
  1019	        if (e.from !== e.to) contraId = e.to;
  1020	      }
  1021	      if (contraId === null) continue;
  1022	      if (_setHas(seenContra, contraId)) continue;
  1023	      _setAdd(seenContra, contraId);
  1024	      _arrPush(contradicting, contraId);
  1025	    }
  1026	    HI.safeArraySort(contradicting, _strcmp);
  1027	    return contradicting;
  1028	  }
  1029	
  1030	  function _countIndependentGroups(matched, graph) {
  1031	    // Map nodeId → correlationGroupId (a node may belong to at most one correlation group). A node
  1032	    // not in any correlation group counts as its own independent unit.
  1033	    var memberToGroupId = _newSet();
  1034	    for (var gi = 0; gi < graph.correlationGroups.length; gi++) {
  1035	      var g = graph.correlationGroups[gi];
  1036	      for (var mi = 0; mi < g.memberNodeIds.length; mi++) {
  1037	        HI.safeDefineDataProperty(memberToGroupId, g.memberNodeIds[mi], g.correlationGroupId);
  1038	      }
  1039	    }
  1040	    var groupSeen = _newSet();
  1041	    var soloCount = 0;
  1042	    var matchedGroupIds = [];
  1043	    for (var i = 0; i < matched.length; i++) {
  1044	      var nid = matched[i].nodeId;
  1045	      var d = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(memberToGroupId, nid);
  1046	      if (d && typeof d.value === 'string') {
  1047	        var gid = d.value;
  1048	        if (!_setHas(groupSeen, gid)) {
  1049	          _setAdd(groupSeen, gid);
  1050	          _arrPush(matchedGroupIds, gid);
  1051	        }
  1052	      } else {
  1053	        soloCount += 1;
  1054	      }
  1055	    }
  1056	    HI.safeArraySort(matchedGroupIds, _strcmp);
  1057	    return { groupIds: matchedGroupIds, soloCount: soloCount, total: matchedGroupIds.length + soloCount };
  1058	  }

  1060	  function _credibilityWeightFor(c) {
  1061	    if (c === 'measured') return 5;
  1062	    if (c === 'derived') return 3;
  1063	    if (c === 'heuristic') return 1;
  1064	    return 0;
  1065	  }
  1066	  function _aggregateCredibility(matched) {
  1067	    var hasMeasured = false, hasDerived = false, hasHeuristic = false, hasSynthetic = false;
  1068	    for (var i = 0; i < matched.length; i++) {
  1069	      var c = matched[i].credibility;
  1070	      if (c === 'measured') hasMeasured = true;
  1071	      else if (c === 'derived') hasDerived = true;
  1072	      else if (c === 'heuristic') hasHeuristic = true;
  1073	      else if (c === 'synthetic') hasSynthetic = true;
  1074	    }
  1075	    if (hasMeasured) return 'Measured';
  1076	    if (hasDerived) return 'Derived';
  1077	    if (hasHeuristic) return 'Heuristic';
  1078	    if (hasSynthetic) return 'Heuristic';
  1079	    return 'Unavailable';
  1080	  }
  1081	  function _classifyConfidence(supportingGroups, soloCount, contradictionCount, supportCount, aggregateCred, rule) {
  1082	    // Determine state:
  1083	    var totalIndependent = supportingGroups + soloCount;
  1084	    // Freshness is handled separately at authority verification (stale nodes rejected via
  1085	    // EVIDENCE_FRESHNESS_STALE) and is NOT a confidence-score input (Codex D3 R5 D3-R5-03
  1086	    // closure). Any node reaching this function is already fresh-enough per policy.
  1087	    var contradictionRatio = (supportCount === 0) ? 0 : (contradictionCount / (supportCount + contradictionCount));
  1088	
  1089	    // Block if cannotConclude reasons present at the graph level; engine adds it externally.
  1090	    // Block if credibility floor not met.
  1091	    if (!_crAtLeast(aggregateCred, rule.minConclusionCredibility)) {
  1092	      return { state: 'low', status: 'inconclusive' };
  1093	    }
  1094	    if (totalIndependent < rule.minIndependentGroups) {
  1095	      return { state: 'insufficient_evidence', status: 'inconclusive' };
   350	        && s.globalArray === _Array

   361	  var _TextEncoder = (typeof TextEncoder !== 'undefined') ? TextEncoder : null;
   362	  var _BufferByteLength = (typeof Buffer !== 'undefined' && Buffer.byteLength) ? Buffer.byteLength : null;

   610	  function _stableStringify(value) { return HI.stableStringify(value); }
   611	
   612	  // FNV-1a 32-bit hash → lowercase hex. Used ONLY as a diagnostic identifier (graphId / correlationGroupId);
   613	  // the actual dedup decisions use the FULL canonical string, not the hash, so a collision in the hash
   614	  // CANNOT cause silent data loss (RN-04 closure). Double-pass widening for nicer-looking ids.
   615	  // Char-code reads go through HI.safeStringCharCodeAt; numeric-to-hex coercion uses
   616	  // HI.safeStringCoerce together with a manual base-16 fold (no Number.prototype.toString ambient
   617	  // call). _strLastN already routes through HI.safeStringSlice.
   618	  function _toHex8(h) {
   619	    var hexChars = '0123456789abcdef';
   620	    var out = '';
   621	    var x = h >>> 0;
   622	    for (var i = 7; i >= 0; i--) {
   623	      var nibble = (x >>> (i * 4)) & 0xF;
   624	      out += hexChars[nibble];
   625	    }
   626	    return out;
   627	  }
   628	  function _hash32(s) {
   629	    var h = 0x811c9dc5;
   630	    var len = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
   631	    for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
   632	      h ^= HI.safeStringCharCodeAt(s, i);
   633	      h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
   634	    }
   635	    return _toHex8(h);
   636	  }
   637	  function _hash64(s) {
   638	    var slen = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
   639	    return _hash32(s) + _hash32(s + '|' + slen);
   640	  }
   641	
   642	  // Semantic canonical string — used as the DEDUP MAP KEY (not a hash). Excludes nodeId so a
   643	  // renamed-id replay collides; excludes freshness so a re-issue at a later timestamp still
   644	  // collides; excludes credibility / provenance / availability (downstream attributes, not the
   645	  // fact). Includes case + session + lap + source identity + category + observation kind/channel/
   646	  // i18nKey/params (sorted JSON). The string IS the key; collisions are impossible by definition.
   647	  function _semanticCanonicalString(node) {
   648	    var id = node.identity;
   649	    var ob = node.observation;
   650	    var payload = {
  1125	      var keptAfterSemantic = [];
  1126	      var rejectedSemanticDuplicates = [];
  1127	      for (var s = 0; s < keptById.length; s++) {

  1131	          _arrPush(rejectedSemanticDuplicates, { fingerprint: _hash64('sem|' + canon), keptNodeId: semSeen[canon], droppedNodeId: nn.nodeId });
  1132	          tally(CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_DUPLICATED_SOURCE_DOUBLECOUNT);

  1141

  1180	          }

  1186	          }

  1188	        }

  1190

  1192	      var groupBucket = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
  1193	      for (var g = 0; g < keptAfterSemantic.length; g++) {

  1204	        var iw = members.length > 0 ? 1 / members.length : 0;

  1207	          memberNodeIds: members,

  1209	        });

  1212	            for (var mj = mi + 1; mj < members.length; mj++) {
  1213	              var from = members[mi] < members[mj] ? members[mi] : members[mj];

   633	    // Codex D3 R5 D3-R5-01 closure: reconstruct the EXACT edge set D2 emits from the
   634	    // sanitized node fields + correlation groups, then require g.edges to match it as a
   635	    // set. D2 emits exactly three edge kinds:
   636	    //   - 'supports'         from each node.supportingEdges entry
   637	    //   - 'contradicts'      from each node.contradictingEdges entry
   638	    //   - 'correlated_with'  from pairs of correlation-group members (sorted lo→hi)
   639	    // The closed enum {supports, contradicts, derived_from, correlated_with, invalidates}
   640	    // describes the universe of LEGAL kinds, but `derived_from` and `invalidates` are
   641	    // RESERVED — no D2 producer emits them. D3 rejects any graph claiming a reserved kind
   642	    // because such a graph cannot have come from D2.
   643	    var EDGE_KIND_EMITTED = ['supports', 'contradicts', 'correlated_with'];
   644	    // Codex D3 R6 D3-R6-03 closure: D2 emits edges WITHOUT deduplication — a node declaring
   645	    // the same id twice in supportingEdges produces 2 identical {from,to,supports} edges in
   646	    // graph.edges. D3 was strictly set-equal which would reject authentic D2 output. Switch
   647	    // to MULTI-SET (count-based) tracking: each declared edge increments expected count;
   648	    // each actual edge decrements; final counts must all be zero.
   649	    var expectedEdgeCount = HI.safeObjectCreateNull();
   650	    function _expectedEdgeKey(from, to, kind) { return from + '|' + to + '|' + kind; }
   651	    function _addExpectedEdge(from, to, kind) {
   652	      var k = _expectedEdgeKey(from, to, kind);
   653	      var d = HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(expectedEdgeCount, k);
   654	      var cur = (d && typeof d.value === 'number') ? d.value : 0;
   655	      HI.safeDefineDataProperty(expectedEdgeCount, k, cur + 1);
   656	    }
   657	    // supports + contradicts from node declarations.

   663	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   664	      }
   665	      if (ne.supportingEdges.length > PER_NODE_EDGE_DECL_CAP) {
   666	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   667	      }
   668	      for (var sj = 0; sj < ne.supportingEdges.length; sj++) {
   669	        var sId = ne.supportingEdges[sj];
   670	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(sId) || !HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, sId)) {
   671	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   672	        }
   673	        if (sId === ne.nodeId) {
   674	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE] };
   675	        }
   676	        _addExpectedEdge(ne.nodeId, sId, 'supports');
   677	      }
   678	      if (!_isPlainArray(ne.contradictingEdges)) {
   679	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID] };
   680	      }
   681	      if (ne.contradictingEdges.length > PER_NODE_EDGE_DECL_CAP) {
   682	        return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED] };
   683	      }
   684	      for (var cj = 0; cj < ne.contradictingEdges.length; cj++) {
   685	        var cId = ne.contradictingEdges[cj];
   686	        if (!_isNonEmptyString(cId) || !HI.safeGetOwnDescriptor(nodeIdSet, cId)) {
   687	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_ORPHAN] };
   688	        }
   689	        if (cId === ne.nodeId) {
   690	          return { valid: false, reasonCodes: [CODES.EVIDENCE_GRAPH_SELF_REFERENCE] };
   691	        }

   693	      }
   694	    }

   696	    for (var cgj = 0; cgj < g.correlationGroups.length; cgj++) {
   697	      var cgM = g.correlationGroups[cgj].memberNodeIds;

   706	    }
   707	    // Verify g.edges matches the expected MULTI-SET — every actual edge consumes one count;
   708	    // any reserved kind / orphan / not-in-expected / over-consumed → reject; final counts

     2	 * tests/r3.0d-hypothesis-engine.test.js — R3.0D D3 · Hypothesis Engine adversarial tests.
     3	 *
     4	 * Sections (per SKYLINE Continuous Resume Directive §7):
     5	 *   A) Functional happy paths — valid graph → hypothesis set; multiple hypotheses; determinism.
     6	 *   B) Structural — output schema, deep-freeze, ID stability, sort order, cap enforcement.
     7	 *   C) Authority — graphId recomputation, deep-frozen requirement, fabricated graph rejection.
     8	 *   D) Scope — wrong case / wrong session, future schema, stale generation token, identity forged.
     9	 *   E) Confidence — duplicate evidence not double-counted, correlation dedup, contradiction
    10	 *      lowers confidence, stale doesn't support, heuristic ≠ measured, low credibility floor.
    11	 *   F) Limitations & cannotConclude — heuristic must declare LIMITATION_HEURISTIC_ONLY; synthetic
    12	 *      must declare LIMITATION_SYNTHETIC_ONLY; empty rule set produces structured cannotConclude.
    13	 *   G) Validation actions + alternative explanations — fixed enum, structured, linked to hyp.
    14	 *   H) Security — Proxy / getter / accessor / Symbol key / non-enumerable / class instance /
    15	 *      nested class / Array subclass / mutated Array.prototype / cyclic object / prototype
    16	 *      pollution / hostile clock / oversized strings / oversized lists / oversized snapshot.
    17	 *   I) Privacy — output contains no raw samples, no paths, no filenames, no private identifiers,
    18	 *      no stack traces.
    19	 *   J) Mutation coverage — each HI.* wrapper used by D3 has a mutation-protected direct test.
    20	 *   K) HI-only audit — output structure does not depend on caller's ambient prototypes.
    21	 *
    22	 * Style: tests print `<message>: <ok|FAIL>` lines so the per-file manifest scanner parses
    23	 * "N passed, M failed" at the bottom (parseAssertions regex in scripts/run-tests-manifest.js).

    34	var pass = 0, fail = 0;
    35	function chk(msg, cond, detail) {
    36	  if (cond) { pass += 1; }
    37	  else { fail += 1; console.log('  FAIL ' + msg + (detail !== undefined ? '  ' + JSON.stringify(detail) : '')); }

    41	var BASE_CLOCK = function () { return '2026-06-29T01:00:00Z'; };
    42	// Default test opts always supply the clock so D3's mandatory freshness policy has a
    43	// reference time. Tests that need to override should construct their own opts.
    44	function _opts(extra) {
    45	  var o = { clock: BASE_CLOCK };
    46	  if (extra) for (var k in extra) o[k] = extra[k];
    47	  return o;
    48	}
    49	// Wrapper so tests get the freshness reference for free; if a test passes its own opts it
    50	// overrides. Use `_he(input, customOpts)` to invoke the engine via the wrapper.
    51	function _he(input, customOpts) {
    52	  return HE.buildHypothesisSet(input, customOpts === undefined ? _opts() : customOpts);
    53	}

    92	function _buildGraph(nodes, caseAss) {

  1369	// ---------- Section J — Mutation coverage (HI wrappers used by D3) -------------------------------
  1370	// We probe the engine's reliance on HI by rebinding ambient prototypes; the engine MUST use captured
  1371	// references and not be affected. The harness uses its own captured Object.defineProperty + Reflect.apply
  1372	// to avoid self-pollution.
  1373	console.log('Section J — HI wrapper mutation resilience');
  1374	var _origObjectDefineProperty = Object.defineProperty;
  1375	var _origReflectApply = Reflect.apply;
  1376	function _runRebind(container, key, hostileImpl, action) {
  1377	  var orig = container[key];
  1378	  var hits = 0;
  1379	  var wrapped = function () { hits += 1; return _origReflectApply(hostileImpl, this, arguments); };
  1380	  _origObjectDefineProperty(container, key, { value: wrapped, configurable: true, writable: true });
  1381	  var result;
  1382	  try { result = action(); }
  1383	  finally {
  1384	    _origObjectDefineProperty(container, key, { value: orig, configurable: true, writable: true });
  1385	  }
  1386	  return { hits: hits, result: result };
  1387	}

  1392	    return _he({ graph: g });
  1393	  });
  1394	  chk('J1: hostile Array.prototype.push never invoked by engine', r.hits === 0);
  1395	  chk('J1: engine still produced valid result', r.result.valid === true);
  1396	})();

     1	/**
     2	 * contracts/r3.0d/evidence-node-contract.js — R3.0D D1 · Contract Foundation (NON-PRODUCTION).
     3	 *
     4	 * Defines the EvidenceNode SHAPE (the smallest unit of fact in the Decision Engine), the closed
     5	 * category enum (directive §8 "Evidence分類"), the Observation sub-shape, and per-node bounded-honesty
     6	 * limitation handling. D1 implements NO graph algorithm — the D2_EVIDENCE_GRAPH service builds + walks
     7	 * the actual graph (dedup, cycle detection, orphan detection, double-counting prevention).
     8	 *
     9	 * Closed key set discipline: any extra own key on a caller-supplied EvidenceNode → UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY.
    10	 * No getter / accessor / Symbol-key / non-enumerable extra reaches the contract; we use
    11	 * Object.keys + descriptor-safe reads to detect every escape attempt.
    12	 *
    13	 * HARDENED-INTRINSICS REFACTOR (2026-06-29): all ambient intrinsic calls routed through
    14	 * hardened-intrinsics.js safe wrappers. Replaced:
    15	 *   - Object.freeze(...)            → HI.deepFreeze (enum tables + every validator return value)
    16	 *   - Object.getPrototypeOf(v)      → HI.safeIsPlainShape (composite plain-object check)
    17	 *   - Array.isArray(v)              → HI.safeIsArray
    18	 *   - Reflect.ownKeys(o)            → HI.safeOwnKeys
    19	 *   - list.indexOf(v) / arr.indexOf → HI.safeArrayIndexOf
    20	 *   - arr.push(x)                   → HI.safeArrayPush
    21	 *   - reasons.push.apply(reasons,a) → HI.safeArrayForEach + HI.safeArrayPush
    22	 *   - reasons.forEach(fn)           → HI.safeArrayForEach
    23	 *   - arr.slice()                   → HI.safeArraySlice
    24	 *   - Number.isInteger              → HI.safeNumberIsInteger
    25	 *   - /regex/.test(s)               → HI.safeRegExpTest
    26	 *   - new TextEncoder().encode().length → HI.safeUtf8ByteLength
    27	 *   - Object.assign({}, params)     → HI.safeObjectAssign on a fresh literal `{}`
    28	 *
    29	 * Plain `{}` / `[]` literal construction is retained — those use [[CreateDataPropertyOrThrow]]
    30	 * (not [[Set]]) and cannot be subverted by prototype setters. Property assignment to those
    31	 * fresh containers is routed through safeDefineDataProperty (inside the safe wrappers).
    32	 *
    33	 * Semantic behaviour preserved exactly: every reason code path, every validator outcome, every
    34	 * Codex D1 R1/R2 defence-in-depth comment is kept verbatim. `_hasOnlyAllowedKeys` still rejects
    35	 * Symbol-keyed + non-enumerable own properties (RN-01 closure).
    36	 *
    37	 * UMD: Node require / Electron renderer global (R3_0D_EvidenceNodeContract).
    38	 */
    39	(function (root) {
    40	  'use strict';
    41	
    42	  function _req(p, g) { var m = null; if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) { try { m = require(p); } catch (e) { m = null; } } return m || (typeof g !== 'undefined' ? g : null); }
    43	  var RC = _req('./reason-codes.js', typeof R3_0D_ReasonCodes !== 'undefined' ? R3_0D_ReasonCodes : undefined);
    44	  var CR = _req('./credibility-contract.js', typeof R3_0D_CredibilityContract !== 'undefined' ? R3_0D_CredibilityContract : undefined);
    45	  var SI = _req('./source-identity-contract.js', typeof R3_0D_SourceIdentityContract !== 'undefined' ? R3_0D_SourceIdentityContract : undefined);
    46	  if (!RC || !CR || !SI) throw new Error('evidence-node-contract.js requires reason-codes + credibility + source-identity contracts');
    47	  var HI = _req('./hardened-intrinsics.js', typeof R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics !== 'undefined' ? R3_0D_HardenedIntrinsics : undefined);
    48	  if (!HI) throw new Error('evidence-node-contract.js requires hardened-intrinsics.js');
    49	  var CODES = RC.REASON_CODES;
    50	
    51	  // Directive §8 "Evidence分類" — closed set. `unknown` is allowed for the rare case where a node is
    52	  // emitted before the category is determined (the priority engine then deprioritises it). New
    53	  // categories must be added here AND to the schema test fixture — no caller-supplied free-form value.
    54	  var EVIDENCE_CATEGORIES = HI.deepFreeze(['data_quality', 'mapping_calibration', 'driver_behaviour', 'vehicle_response', 'setup_model', 'unknown']);
    55	
    56	  // EvidenceNode closed key set (D1 SHAPE).
    57	  var EVIDENCE_NODE_KEYS = HI.deepFreeze([
    58	    'nodeId',
    59	    'category',
    60	    'identity',          // SourceIdentity (caseId, sessionId, lapId?, sourceId, sourceVersion, freshness)
    61	    'credibility',       // EVIDENCE_CREDIBILITY enum
    62	    'provenance',        // PROVENANCE enum
    63	    'availability',      // AVAILABILITY enum
    64	    'confidence',        // D1: { state: 'unresolved' | 'not_computed' } only
    65	    'observation',       // structured observation payload (closed schema below)
    66	    'limitations',       // array of LIMITATION_* codes (bounded honesty)
    67	    'supportingEdges',   // array of nodeId strings (other evidence supporting this one)
    68	    'contradictingEdges',// array of nodeId strings (other evidence contradicting this one)
    69	    'schemaVersion',     // integer; rejects future schemas at D1
    70	  ]);
    71	
    72	  // Observation closed key set. The observation is the FACT this node carries (e.g. a metric value,
    73	  // a flag that a channel is missing). The shape is intentionally minimal at D1 — domain-specific
    74	  // fields belong to category-specific specialisations introduced at D2 / D3.
    75	  var OBSERVATION_KEYS = HI.deepFreeze(['kind', 'i18nKey', 'params', 'channel']);
    76	  var OBSERVATION_KIND_ALLOWED = HI.deepFreeze(['metric_value', 'metric_threshold_crossed', 'channel_missing', 'channel_uncalibrated', 'channel_partial', 'lap_authority_blocked', 'comparison_blocked', 'qualitative_marker']);
    77	
    78	  // Schema version pin. A node whose schemaVersion is HIGHER than D1's SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION is
    79	  // rejected with UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA — a future-schema node must NEVER be silently accepted.
    80	  var SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;
    81	
    82	  // Caps. Directive §8: "array caps, graph caps, total envelope cap".
    83	  var LIMITATIONS_ARRAY_CAP = 32;
    84	  var EDGES_ARRAY_CAP = 64;
    85	  var STRING_BYTE_CAP = 512;     // per-field
    86	  var PARAMS_VALUE_BYTE_CAP = 256;
    87	
    88	  // NodeId grammar. Directive: "no private path, no filename, no raw telemetry, deterministic / secure
    89	  // immutable id, caller cannot collide". The contract enforces a grammar (no '/', no '..', no '\\',
    90	  // no leading '.'); production-side D2 layer adds collision detection.
    91	  var NODE_ID_RE = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._-]{0,127}$/;
    92	  var NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE = /(\.\.|[\/\\]|^\.)/;
    93	
    94	  // _isPlain(v) — routed through HI.safeIsPlainShape so the Object.prototype identity check uses
    95	  // hardened-intrinsics' captured _ObjectPrototype reference (no ambient lookup).
    96	  function _isPlain(v) { return HI.safeIsPlainShape(v) === 'plain-object'; }
    97	  function _nonEmptyStr(v) { return typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0; }
    98	  // Codex D1 R1 Finding RN-01 closure: Reflect.ownKeys catches non-enumerable + Symbol-keyed.
    99	  // Now via HI.safeOwnKeys (captured Reflect.ownKeys behind Reflect.apply — no rebind).
   100	  function _hasOnlyAllowedKeys(o, allowed) {
   101	    var keys = HI.safeOwnKeys(o);
   102	    if (keys === null) return false;
   103	    for (var i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
   104	      var k = keys[i];
   105	      if (typeof k === 'symbol') return false;
   106	      if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(allowed, k) === -1) return false;
   107	    }
   108	    return true;
   109	  }
   110	  function _isFiniteNum(v) { return typeof v === 'number' && v === v && v !== Infinity && v !== -Infinity; }
   111	  // UTF-8 byte width via captured TextEncoder (manual fallback inside HI handles the no-TextEncoder
   112	  // path — see hardened-intrinsics.js safeUtf8ByteLength).
   113	  function _utf8Bytes(s) { return HI.safeUtf8ByteLength(s); }
   114	  function _isStringCodeArray(v, cap) {
   115	    if (!HI.safeIsArray(v) || v.length > cap) return false;
   116	    for (var i = 0; i < v.length; i++) if (typeof v[i] !== 'string' || v[i].length === 0) return false;
   117	    return true;
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  /**
   121	   * validateObservationShape(o) — closed-key plain object with kind ∈ OBSERVATION_KIND_ALLOWED,
   122	   * i18nKey non-empty string (no UI prose at D1), optional params plain object whose own values are
   123	   * finite numbers, booleans, null, or short strings (≤ PARAMS_VALUE_BYTE_CAP).
   124	   */
   125	  function validateObservationShape(o) {
   126	    // RN-02 closure: outer try/catch.
   127	    try {
   128	    if (!_isPlain(o)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation not a plain object' });
   129	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(o, OBSERVATION_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   130	    if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(OBSERVATION_KIND_ALLOWED, o.kind) === -1) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.kind not allowed' });
   131	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(o.i18nKey)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.i18nKey missing' });
   132	    if (_utf8Bytes(o.i18nKey) > STRING_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]);
   133	    if ('channel' in o && o.channel !== null && !_nonEmptyStr(o.channel)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID]);
   134	    if ('params' in o && o.params !== null && o.params !== undefined) {
   135	      if (!_isPlain(o.params)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID]);
   136	      // Codex D1 R2 Finding RN-07 closure: Reflect.ownKeys + Symbol rejection mirrors validateParamsShape.
   137	      // Now via HI.safeOwnKeys (captured Reflect.ownKeys + Reflect.apply, not rebindable).
   138	      var pk = HI.safeOwnKeys(o.params);
   139	      if (pk === null) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID]);
   140	      for (var i = 0; i < pk.length; i++) {
   141	        var k = pk[i];
   142	        if (typeof k === 'symbol') return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   143	        var v = o.params[k];
   144	        if (v === null || typeof v === 'boolean') continue;
   145	        if (typeof v === 'number') { if (!_isFiniteNum(v)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.NUMERIC_INVALID]); continue; }
   146	        if (typeof v === 'string') { if (_utf8Bytes(v) > PARAMS_VALUE_BYTE_CAP) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED]); continue; }
   147	        return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID], { detail: 'observation.params value type not allowed' });
   148	      }
   149	    }
   150	    return HI.deepFreeze({ valid: true });
   151	    } catch (e) {
   152	      return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID, CODES.INTERNAL_CONTRACT_VIOLATION], { detail: 'observation validator threw on hostile input' });
   153	    }
   154	  }
   155	
   156	  /**
   157	   * validateEvidenceNodeShape(n) — D1 STRUCTURAL gate. Composes credibility, source-identity, and
   158	   * observation validators. Returns { valid:true } or buildBlockedResult.
   159	   *
   160	   * D1 does NOT validate the graph relations (cycles, orphans, double-counting) — that is the D2
   161	   * EVIDENCE_GRAPH service's job. D1 ONLY proves a single node's STRUCTURE.
   162	   */
   163	  function validateEvidenceNodeShape(nIn) {
   164	    // Outer try/catch + Codex D1 R2 RN-06 Proxy-rejection input clone.
   165	    try {
   166	    if (!RC.isOriginalPlainObject(nIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'node prototype is not Object.prototype or null (class instance / Proxy / non-plain rejected pre-clone)' });
   167	    if (RC.hasHiddenOwnKey(nIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY], { detail: 'node carries Symbol-keyed or non-enumerable own property' });
   168	    if (RC.hasNonPlainNestedObject(nIn)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.PROTOTYPE_POLLUTION_REJECTED], { detail: 'node contains a nested non-plain object (class instance laundered through identity / confidence / observation)' });
   169	    var n = RC.toCleanCopy(nIn);
   170	    if (!_isPlain(n)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID], { detail: 'node not a plain object (or proxy/non-cloneable rejected)' });
   171	    if (!_hasOnlyAllowedKeys(n, EVIDENCE_NODE_KEYS)) return RC.buildBlockedResult([CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID, CODES.UNKNOWN_OWN_KEY]);
   172	    var reasons = [];
   173	
   174	    // schemaVersion: must be the integer SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION. Higher → UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA.
   175	    if (!HI.safeNumberIsInteger(n.schemaVersion)) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID);
   176	    else if (n.schemaVersion > SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.UNSUPPORTED_FUTURE_SCHEMA);
   177	    else if (n.schemaVersion < 1) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID);
   178	
   179	    // nodeId
   180	    if (!_nonEmptyStr(n.nodeId)) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_MISSING_ID);
   181	    else if (HI.safeRegExpTest(NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN_RE, n.nodeId) || !HI.safeRegExpTest(NODE_ID_RE, n.nodeId)) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_ID_FORBIDDEN);
   182	
   183	    // category
   184	    if (HI.safeArrayIndexOf(EVIDENCE_CATEGORIES, n.category) === -1) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_CATEGORY_UNKNOWN);
   185	
   186	    // identity
   187	    var idCheck = SI.validateSourceIdentity(n.identity);
   188	    if (idCheck.valid !== true) {
   189	      var idCodes = idCheck.reasonCodes;
   190	      if (HI.safeIsArray(idCodes)) {
   191	        HI.safeArrayForEach(idCodes, function (c) { HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, c); });
   192	      } else {
   193	        HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.SOURCE_IDENTITY_INVALID);
   194	      }
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    // credibility, provenance, availability
   198	    var crCheck = CR.validateEvidenceCredibility(n.credibility);
   199	    if (crCheck.valid !== true) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_CREDIBILITY_INVALID);
   200	    var prCheck = CR.validateProvenance(n.provenance);
   201	    if (prCheck.valid !== true) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_PROVENANCE_INVALID);
   202	    var avCheck = CR.validateAvailability(n.availability);
   203	    if (avCheck.valid !== true) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID);
   204	
   205	    // confidence (D1: caller cannot supply numeric)
   206	    var cfCheck = CR.validateConfidenceShape(n.confidence);
   207	    if (cfCheck.valid !== true) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_CONFIDENCE_FORBIDDEN);
   208	
   209	    // observation — propagate inner reasonCodes so NUMERIC_INVALID / BYTE_CAP_EXCEEDED surface to the caller
   210	    var obCheck = validateObservationShape(n.observation);
   211	    if (obCheck.valid !== true) {
   212	      HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_OBSERVATION_INVALID);
   213	      if (HI.safeIsArray(obCheck.reasonCodes)) {
   214	        HI.safeArrayForEach(obCheck.reasonCodes, function (c) { HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, c); });
   215	      }
   216	    }
   217	
   218	    // limitations array — closed code list, bounded length
   219	    if (!_isStringCodeArray(n.limitations, LIMITATIONS_ARRAY_CAP)) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED);
   220	    else {
   221	      for (var li = 0; li < n.limitations.length; li++) {
   222	        var lc = n.limitations[li];
   223	        if (!RC.isReasonCode(lc)) { HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.EVIDENCE_NODE_INVALID); break; }
   224	      }
   225	    }
   226	
   227	    // edges arrays
   228	    if (!_isStringCodeArray(n.supportingEdges, EDGES_ARRAY_CAP)) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED);
   229	    if (!_isStringCodeArray(n.contradictingEdges, EDGES_ARRAY_CAP)) HI.safeArrayPush(reasons, CODES.ARRAY_CAP_EXCEEDED);
   230	
   231	    // self-reference (D1 catches the easy case; D2 catches deep cycles)
   232	    if (_nonEmptyStr(n.nodeId)) {
   233	      var supEdges = HI.safeIsArray(n.supportingEdges) ? n.supportingEdges : [];

  1118	// Reuse D2/D3's FNV-1a 32-bit-twice hash to construct HASH-VALID test fixtures (graphs whose
  1119	// `graphId` field matches the recomputed value on their actual contents). Without this we can
  1120	// only test rejection-via-hash-mismatch, which doesn't exercise downstream checks.
  1121	console.log('Section H6 — Codex D3 R6 closures (hash-valid fixtures)');
  1122	function _hash32_for_test(s) {
  1123	  var h = 0x811c9dc5;
  1124	  var len = (typeof s === 'string') ? s.length : 0;
  1125	  for (var i = 0; i < len; i++) {
  1126	    h ^= s.charCodeAt(i);
  1127	    h = (h + ((h << 1) + (h << 4) + (h << 7) + (h << 8) + (h << 24))) >>> 0;
  1128	  }
  1129	  var hexChars = '0123456789abcdef';
  1130	  var out = '';
  1131	  for (var j = 7; j >= 0; j--) out += hexChars[(h >>> (j * 4)) & 0xF];
  1132	  return out;
  1133	}
  1134	function _hash64_for_test(s) {
  1135	  return _hash32_for_test(s) + _hash32_for_test(s + '|' + s.length);
  1136	}
